// (Contains ONLY pseudo-source library objects)
Version: 1


// *************************************** //
// Library objects for pseudo-source setup //
// *************************************** //

Ptype: MassTransferCoefficient
Description: [[Mass transfer coefficient for diffusion of chemical between air and surface soil compartments;
root zone soil and surface soil compartments; root zone soil and vadose zone soil compartments; and within the
vadose zone soil compartment]]
DataType: FloatingPoint
Units: m/day


// Algorithms for distributing mass from pseudo-sources to leaf/soil/water

Compartment: DryParticleSource
    Property: acceptableAbiotic
        Value: <Unset>
    Property: category
        Value: PseudoSource | Dry | Particle
    Property: concentrationOutputFactor
        Value: 1.0
    Property: concentrationOutputUnits
        Value: <Unset>
    Property: isBiotic
        Value: false

Compartment: WetParticleSource
    Property: acceptableAbiotic
        Value: <Unset>
    Property: category
        Value: PseudoSource | Wet | Particle
    Property: concentrationOutputFactor
        Value: 1.0
    Property: concentrationOutputUnits
        Value: <Unset>
    Property: isBiotic
        Value: false

Compartment: DryVaporSource
    Property: acceptableAbiotic
        Value: <Unset>
    Property: category
        Value: PseudoSource | Dry | Vapor
    Property: concentrationOutputFactor
        Value: 1.0
    Property: concentrationOutputUnits
        Value: <Unset>
    Property: isBiotic
        Value: false

Compartment: WetVaporSource
    Property: acceptableAbiotic
        Value: <Unset>
    Property: category
        Value: PseudoSource | Wet | Vapor
    Property: concentrationOutputFactor
        Value: 1.0
    Property: concentrationOutputUnits
        Value: <Unset>
    Property: isBiotic
        Value: false

// for specification of deposition rates instead of emission rates ------------
PType: surfaceDepositionRate
    DataType: FloatingPoint
    DefaultValue: 0.0
    Description: [[ The externally-derived deposition rate for a pseudo-source ]]
    Units: g/m**2/day

PType: surfaceDepositionArea
    DataType: FloatingPoint
    DefaultValue: 0.0
    Description: [[ The surface area upon which mass from a pseudo-souce is deposited ]]
    Units: m**2

// water ----------------------------------------------------------------------
Algorithm: Direct Transfer from PseudoSource to Surface water
    Property: category
        Value: Abstract transfer
    Property: chemicalCategory
        Value: All
    Property: doesTransformChemical
        Value: false
    Property: doesTransportChemical
        Value: true
    Property: enabled
        Value: true
    Property: isDefaultForCategory
        Value: true
    Property: receivingCompartmentCategory
        Value: Abiotic | Surface water | Surface water - Default
    Property: sendingCompartmentCategory
        Value: PseudoSource

    Property: transferFactor
        Value: 1.0
        Description: [[ Deposition to water parcels goes directly to the water. ]]



// particles ------------------------------------------------------------------

Algorithm: Dry Deposition of Particles from DryParticleSource to Plants
    Property: category
        Value: Abstract transfer
    Property: chemicalCategory
        Value: All
    Property: doesTransformChemical
        Value: false
    Property: doesTransportChemical
        Value: true
    Property: enabled
        Value: true
    Property: isDefaultForCategory
        Value: true
    Property: receivingCompartmentCategory
        Value: Terrestrial Plant | Leaf Particle
    Property: sendingCompartmentCategory
        Value: PseudoSource | Dry | Particle

    Property: transferFactor
        Form: formula
        Value: [[ 
            receivingVolumeElementSumOf[Terrestrial Plant | Leaf].AllowExchange_forAir
            * 
            receivingVolumeElementSumOf[Terrestrial Plant | Leaf].DryDepInterceptionFraction
            ]]
        Description: [[ Based on Algorithm: Dry Deposition of Particles from Air to Plants ]]

Algorithm: Dry Deposition of Particles from DryParticleSource to Soil
    Property: category
        Value: Abstract transfer
    Property: chemicalCategory
        Value: All
    Property: doesTransformChemical
        Value: false
    Property: doesTransportChemical
        Value: true
    Property: enabled
        Value: true
    Property: isDefaultForCategory
        Value: true
    Property: receivingCompartmentCategory
        Value: Abiotic | Soil | Surface Soil | Surface Soil - Default
    Property: sendingCompartmentCategory
        Value: PseudoSource | Dry | Particle

    Property: transferFactor
        Form: formula
        Value: [[ 1 - (
                receivingVolumeElementSumOf[Terrestrial Plant | Leaf].AllowExchange_forAir 
                * 
                receivingVolumeElementSumOf[Terrestrial Plant | Leaf].DryDepInterceptionFraction
                ) ]]
        Description: [[ Based on Algorithm: Dry Deposition of Particles from Air to Soil(AlgInstID_2490) ]]


Algorithm: Wet Deposition of Particles from WetParticleSource to Plants
    Property: category
        Value: Abstract transfer
    Property: chemicalCategory
        Value: All
    Property: doesTransformChemical
        Value: false
    Property: doesTransportChemical
        Value: true
    Property: enabled
        Value: true
    Property: isDefaultForCategory
        Value: true
    Property: receivingCompartmentCategory
        Value: Terrestrial Plant | Leaf Particle
    Property: sendingCompartmentCategory
        Value: PseudoSource | Wet | Particle

    Property: transferFactor
        Form: formula
        Value: [[ receivingVolumeElementSumOf[Terrestrial Plant | Leaf].AllowExchange_forAir 
                * 
                receivingVolumeElementSumOf[Terrestrial Plant | Leaf].WetDepInterceptionFraction 
                ]]
        Description: [[ Based on Algorithm: Wet Deposition of Particles from Air to Plants ]]


Algorithm: Wet Deposition of Particles from WetParticleSource to Surface Soil
    Property: category
        Value: Abstract transfer
    Property: chemicalCategory
        Value: All
    Property: doesTransformChemical
        Value: false
    Property: doesTransportChemical
        Value: true
    Property: enabled
        Value: true
    Property: isDefaultForCategory
        Value: true
    Property: receivingCompartmentCategory
        Value: Abiotic | Soil | Surface Soil | Surface Soil - Default
    Property: sendingCompartmentCategory
        Value: PseudoSource | Wet | Particle

    Property: transferFactor
        Form: formula
        Value: [[ 1 - (
                receivingVolumeElementSumOf[Terrestrial Plant | Leaf].AllowExchange_forAir 
                * 
                receivingVolumeElementSumOf[Terrestrial Plant | Leaf].WetDepInterceptionFraction
                ) ]]
        Description: [[ Based on Algorithm: Wet Deposition of Particles to Surface Soil(AlgInstID_2495) ]]



// Hg2 Dry deposition of vapor ------------------------------------------------

Algorithm: Dry Deposition of Vapor from DryVaporSource to Plants,  Hg2
    Property: category
        Value: Abstract transfer
    Property: receivingChemicalName
        Value: Divalent Mercury
    Property: sendingChemicalName
        Value: Divalent Mercury
    Property: doesTransformChemical
        Value: false
    Property: doesTransportChemical
        Value: true
    Property: enabled
        Value: true
    Property: isDefaultForCategory
        Value: true
    Property: receivingCompartmentCategory
        Value: Terrestrial Plant | Leaf
    Property: sendingCompartmentCategory
        Value: PseudoSource | Dry | Vapor

    Property: transferFactor
        Form: formula
        Value: [[ 
            receivingCompartment.AllowExchange_forAir
            * 
            receivingCompartment.DryDepInterceptionFraction
            ]]
        Description: [[ Based on Algorithm: Dry Deposition of Vapor from Air to Plants ]]

Algorithm: Dry Deposition of Vapor from DryVaporSource to Soil, Hg2
    Property: category
        Value: Abstract transfer
    Property: receivingChemicalName
        Value: Divalent Mercury
    Property: sendingChemicalName
        Value: Divalent Mercury
    Property: doesTransformChemical
        Value: false
    Property: doesTransportChemical
        Value: true
    Property: enabled
        Value: true
    Property: isDefaultForCategory
        Value: true
    Property: receivingCompartmentCategory
        Value: Abiotic | Soil | Surface Soil | Surface Soil - Default
    Property: sendingCompartmentCategory
        Value: PseudoSource | Dry | Vapor

    Property: transferFactor
        Form: formula
        Value: [[ 
            1 - (
            receivingVolumeElementSumOf[Terrestrial Plant | Leaf].AllowExchange_forAir
            * 
            receivingVolumeElementSumOf[Terrestrial Plant | Leaf].DryDepInterceptionFraction
            )
            ]]
        Description: [[ Based on 
            Algorithm: Dry Deposition of Vapor from Air to Soil(AlgInstID_2488)
            ]]



// declare intermediate vars --------------------------------------------------

PType: transferFractionLeaf
    DataType: FloatingPoint
    DefaultValue: 0.0
    Units: <Unset>
    Description: [[ Intermediate variable for PseudoSource simulated diffusion algorithms ]]

PType: transferFractionSoil
    DataType: FloatingPoint
    DefaultValue: 0.0
    Units: <Unset>
    Description: [[ Intermediate variable for PseudoSource simulated diffusion algorithms ]]

PType: transferFractionTotal
    DataType: FloatingPoint
    DefaultValue: 0.0
    Units: <Unset>
    Description: [[ Sum of transferFractionLeaf and transferFractionSoil ]]



// Dry vapor to leaf ----------------------------------------------------------

Algorithm: Diffusion from DryVaporSource to Plant Leaf, Hg0
    Property: category
        Value: Abstract transfer

    Property: receivingChemicalName
        Value: Elemental Mercury
    Property: sendingChemicalName
        Value: Elemental Mercury

    Property: doesTransformChemical
        Value: false
    Property: doesTransportChemical
        Value: true
    Property: enabled
        Value: true
    Property: isDefaultForCategory
        Value: true
    Property: receivingCompartmentCategory
        Value: Terrestrial Plant | Leaf
    Property: sendingCompartmentCategory
        Value: PseudoSource | Dry | Vapor
    Property: MassTransferCoefficient
        Form: formula
        Value: [[ receivingVolumeElementSumOf[Abiotic | Soil | Surface Soil | Surface Soil - Default].Chemical.MassTransferCoefficientOnAirSideofAirSoilBoundary ]]
        Description: [[ Copied from Algorithm: Diffusion from Air to Surface Soil, Hg0(AlgInstID_2507) ]]

    Property: transferFractionLeaf
        Form: formula
        Value: [[ 
        ReceivingCompartment.AllowExchange_forAir *  
            (
                (receivingVolumeElementSumOf[Abiotic | Soil | Surface Soil | Surface Soil - Default].Area * 2*ReceivingCompartment.LeafAreaIndex * ReceivingCompartment.Chemical.TotalCuticularConductance)
                + 
                (ReceivingCompartment.LeafAreaIndex * receivingVolumeElementSumOf[Abiotic | Soil | Surface Soil | Surface Soil - Default].Area * (ReceivingCompartment.isDay_forAir * ReceivingCompartment.Chemical.TotalStomatalConductance))
            )
        ]]
        Description: [[ Based on Algorithm: Diffusion from Air to Plant Leaf, Hg0, Default(Riederer 1995) ]]

    Property: transferFractionSoil
        Form: formula
        Value: [[ 
        (
            (receivingVolumeElementSumOf[Abiotic | Soil | Surface Soil | Surface Soil - Default].FractionofAreaAvailableforVerticalDiffusion * receivingVolumeElementSumOf[Abiotic | Soil | Surface Soil | Surface Soil - Default].Area)
            / 
            SendingChemical.Z_pureAir

        ) * (
            (1/(SendingChemical.Z_pureAir * Algorithm.MassTransferCoefficient))
            + 
            (1/(receivingVolumeElementSumOf[Abiotic | Soil | Surface Soil | Surface Soil - Default].Chemical.Z_Total * (receivingVolumeElementSumOf[Abiotic | Soil | Surface Soil | Surface Soil - Default].Chemical.D_effective / receivingVolumeElementSumOf[Abiotic | Soil | Surface Soil | Surface Soil - Default].Depth)))

        )**(-1)
        ]]
        Description: [[ Based on Algorithm: Diffusion from Air to Surface Soil, Hg0(AlgInstID_2507) ]]

    Property: transferFractionTotal
        Form: formula
        Value: [[ Algorithm.transferFractionLeaf + Algorithm.transferFractionSoil ]]

    Property: transferFactor
        Form: formula
        Value: [[ Algorithm.transferFractionTotal > 0 ? Algorithm.transferFractionLeaf / Algorithm.transferFractionTotal : 0 ]]
        Description: [[ Based on 
            Algorithm: Diffusion from Air to Plant Leaf, Hg0, Default(Riederer 1995)
            and 
            Algorithm: Diffusion from Air to Surface Soil, Hg0(AlgInstID_2507) 
            ]]

Algorithm: Diffusion from DryVaporSource to Plant Leaf, MHg
    Property: category
        Value: Abstract transfer

    Property: receivingChemicalName
        Value: MethylMercury
    Property: sendingChemicalName
        Value: MethylMercury

    Property: doesTransformChemical
        Value: false
    Property: doesTransportChemical
        Value: true
    Property: enabled
        Value: true
    Property: isDefaultForCategory
        Value: true
    Property: receivingCompartmentCategory
        Value: Terrestrial Plant | Leaf
    Property: sendingCompartmentCategory
        Value: PseudoSource | Dry | Vapor
    Property: MassTransferCoefficient
        Form: formula
        Value: [[ receivingVolumeElementSumOf[Abiotic | Soil | Surface Soil | Surface Soil - Default].Chemical.MassTransferCoefficientOnAirSideofAirSoilBoundary ]]
        Description: [[ Copied from Algorithm: Diffusion from Air to Surface Soil, Hg0(AlgInstID_2507) ]]

    Property: transferFractionLeaf
        Form: formula
        Value: [[ 
        ReceivingCompartment.AllowExchange_forAir *  
            (
                (receivingVolumeElementSumOf[Abiotic | Soil | Surface Soil | Surface Soil - Default].Area * 2*ReceivingCompartment.LeafAreaIndex * ReceivingCompartment.Chemical.TotalCuticularConductance)
                + 
                (ReceivingCompartment.LeafAreaIndex * receivingVolumeElementSumOf[Abiotic | Soil | Surface Soil | Surface Soil - Default].Area * (ReceivingCompartment.isDay_forAir * ReceivingCompartment.Chemical.TotalStomatalConductance))
            )
        ]]
        Description: [[ Based on 
                    Algorithm: Diffusion from Air to Plant Leaf, MHg, Default(Riederer 1995)
                    ]]

    Property: transferFractionSoil
        Form: formula
        Value: [[ 
        (
            (receivingVolumeElementSumOf[Abiotic | Soil | Surface Soil | Surface Soil - Default].FractionofAreaAvailableforVerticalDiffusion * receivingVolumeElementSumOf[Abiotic | Soil | Surface Soil | Surface Soil - Default].Area)
            / 
            SendingChemical.Z_pureAir

        ) * (
            (1/(SendingChemical.Z_pureAir * Algorithm.MassTransferCoefficient))
            + 
            (1/(receivingVolumeElementSumOf[Abiotic | Soil | Surface Soil | Surface Soil - Default].Chemical.Z_Total * (receivingVolumeElementSumOf[Abiotic | Soil | Surface Soil | Surface Soil - Default].Chemical.D_effective / receivingVolumeElementSumOf[Abiotic | Soil | Surface Soil | Surface Soil - Default].Depth)))

        )**(-1)
        ]]
        Description: [[ Based on 
            Algorithm: Diffusion from Air to Surface Soil, MHg(AlgInstID_2509) 
            ]]

    Property: transferFractionTotal
        Form: formula
        Value: [[ Algorithm.transferFractionLeaf + Algorithm.transferFractionSoil ]]

    Property: transferFactor
        Form: formula
        Value: [[ Algorithm.transferFractionTotal > 0 ? Algorithm.transferFractionLeaf / Algorithm.transferFractionTotal : 0 ]]
        Description: [[ Based on 
            Algorithm: Diffusion from Air to Plant Leaf, MHg, Default(Riederer 1995)
            and 
            Algorithm: Diffusion from Air to Surface Soil, MHg(AlgInstID_2509) 
            ]]

Algorithm: Diffusion from DryVaporSource to Plant Leaf, Organics
    Property: category
        Value: Abstract transfer

    Property: chemicalCategory
        Value: Organic

    Property: doesTransformChemical
        Value: false
    Property: doesTransportChemical
        Value: true
    Property: enabled
        Value: true
    Property: isDefaultForCategory
        Value: true
    Property: receivingCompartmentCategory
        Value: Terrestrial Plant | Leaf
    Property: sendingCompartmentCategory
        Value: PseudoSource | Dry | Vapor
    Property: MassTransferCoefficient
        Form: formula
        Value: [[ receivingVolumeElementSumOf[Abiotic | Soil | Surface Soil | Surface Soil - Default].Chemical.MassTransferCoefficientOnAirSideofAirSoilBoundary ]]
        Description: [[ Copied from Algorithm: Diffusion from Air to Surface Soil, Hg0(AlgInstID_2507) ]]

    Property: transferFractionLeaf
        Form: formula
        Value: [[ 
        ReceivingCompartment.AllowExchange_forAir *  
            (
                (receivingVolumeElementSumOf[Abiotic | Soil | Surface Soil | Surface Soil - Default].Area * 2*ReceivingCompartment.LeafAreaIndex * ReceivingCompartment.Chemical.TotalCuticularConductance)
                + 
                (ReceivingCompartment.LeafAreaIndex * receivingVolumeElementSumOf[Abiotic | Soil | Surface Soil | Surface Soil - Default].Area * (ReceivingCompartment.isDay_forAir * ReceivingCompartment.Chemical.TotalStomatalConductance))
            )
        ]]
        Description: [[ Based on 
                    Algorithm: Diffusion from Air to Plant Leaf, Organics, Default(Riederer 1995)
                    ]]

    Property: transferFractionSoil
        Form: formula
        Value: [[ 
        (
            (receivingVolumeElementSumOf[Abiotic | Soil | Surface Soil | Surface Soil - Default].FractionofAreaAvailableforVerticalDiffusion * receivingVolumeElementSumOf[Abiotic | Soil | Surface Soil | Surface Soil - Default].Area)
            / 
            SendingChemical.Z_pureAir

        ) * (
            (1/(SendingChemical.Z_pureAir * Algorithm.MassTransferCoefficient))
            + 
            (1/(receivingVolumeElementSumOf[Abiotic | Soil | Surface Soil | Surface Soil - Default].Chemical.Z_Total * (receivingVolumeElementSumOf[Abiotic | Soil | Surface Soil | Surface Soil - Default].Chemical.D_effective / receivingVolumeElementSumOf[Abiotic | Soil | Surface Soil | Surface Soil - Default].Depth)))

        )**(-1)
        ]]
        Description: [[ Based on 
            Algorithm: Diffusion from Air to Surface Soil, Organics(AlgInstID_2505) 
            ]]

    Property: transferFractionTotal
        Form: formula
        Value: [[ Algorithm.transferFractionLeaf + Algorithm.transferFractionSoil ]]

    Property: transferFactor
        Form: formula
        Value: [[ Algorithm.transferFractionTotal > 0 ? Algorithm.transferFractionLeaf / Algorithm.transferFractionTotal : 0 ]]
        Description: [[ Based on 
            Algorithm: Diffusion from Air to Plant Leaf, Organics, Default(Riederer 1995)
            and 
            Algorithm: Diffusion from Air to Surface Soil, Organics(AlgInstID_2505)
            ]]


// Dry vapor to soil ----------------------------------------------------------

Algorithm: Diffusion from DryVaporSource to Surface Soil, Hg0
    Property: category
        Value: Abstract transfer

    Property: receivingChemicalName
        Value: Elemental Mercury
    Property: sendingChemicalName
        Value: Elemental Mercury

    Property: doesTransformChemical
        Value: false
    Property: doesTransportChemical
        Value: true
    Property: enabled
        Value: true
    Property: isDefaultForCategory
        Value: true
    Property: receivingCompartmentCategory
        Value: Abiotic | Soil | Surface Soil | Surface Soil - Default
    Property: sendingCompartmentCategory
        Value: PseudoSource | Dry | Vapor
    Property: MassTransferCoefficient
        Form: formula
        Value: [[ ReceivingCompartment.Chemical.MassTransferCoefficientOnAirSideofAirSoilBoundary ]]
        Description: [[ Copied from Algorithm: Diffusion from Air to Surface Soil, Hg0(AlgInstID_2507) ]]

    Property: transferFractionLeaf
        Form: formula
        Value: [[ 
        receivingVolumeElementSumOf[Terrestrial Plant | Leaf].AllowExchange_forAir *  
            (
                (receivingCompartment.Area * 2*receivingVolumeElementSumOf[Terrestrial Plant | Leaf].LeafAreaIndex * receivingVolumeElementSumOf[Terrestrial Plant | Leaf].Chemical.TotalCuticularConductance)
                + 
                (receivingVolumeElementSumOf[Terrestrial Plant | Leaf].LeafAreaIndex * receivingCompartment.Area * (receivingVolumeElementSumOf[Terrestrial Plant | Leaf].isDay_forAir * receivingVolumeElementSumOf[Terrestrial Plant | Leaf].Chemical.TotalStomatalConductance))
            )
        ]]
        Description: [[ Based on 
            Algorithm: Diffusion from Air to Plant Leaf, Hg0, Default(Riederer 1995) 
            ]]

    Property: transferFractionSoil
        Form: formula
        Value: [[ 
        (
            (receivingCompartment.FractionofAreaAvailableforVerticalDiffusion * receivingCompartment.Area)
            / 
            SendingChemical.Z_pureAir

        ) * (
            (1/(SendingChemical.Z_pureAir * Algorithm.MassTransferCoefficient))
            + 
            (1/(receivingCompartment.Chemical.Z_Total * (receivingCompartment.Chemical.D_effective / receivingCompartment.Depth)))

        )**(-1)
        ]]
        Description: [[ Based on 
            Algorithm: Diffusion from Air to Surface Soil, Hg0(AlgInstID_2507) 
            ]]

    Property: transferFractionTotal
        Form: formula
        Value: [[ Algorithm.transferFractionLeaf + Algorithm.transferFractionSoil ]]

    Property: transferFactor
        Form: formula
        Value: [[ Algorithm.transferFractionTotal > 0 ? Algorithm.transferFractionSoil / Algorithm.transferFractionTotal : 0 ]]
        Description: [[ Based on 
            Algorithm: Diffusion from Air to Plant Leaf, Hg0, Default(Riederer 1995) 
            and 
            Algorithm: Diffusion from Air to Surface Soil, Hg0(AlgInstID_2507) 
            ]]

// Wet vapor to plants --------------------------------------------------------

Algorithm: Wet Deposition of Vapor Phase from WetVaporSource to Plants,  Hg0
    Property: category
        Value: Abstract transfer

    Property: receivingChemicalName
        Value: Elemental Mercury
    Property: sendingChemicalName
        Value: Elemental Mercury

    Property: doesTransformChemical
        Value: false
    Property: doesTransportChemical
        Value: true
    Property: enabled
        Value: true
    Property: isDefaultForCategory
        Value: true
    Property: receivingCompartmentCategory
        Value: Terrestrial Plant | Leaf
    Property: sendingCompartmentCategory
        Value: PseudoSource | Wet | Vapor

    Property: transferFactor
        Form: formula
        Value: [[ 
            receivingCompartment.AllowExchange_forAir
            * 
            receivingCompartment.WetDepInterceptionFraction
            ]]
        Description: [[ Based on 
            Algorithm: Wet Deposition of Vapor Phase to Plant Leaf from Air, Hg0(AlgInstID_2512) 
            ]]

Algorithm: Wet Deposition of Vapor Phase from WetVaporSource to Plants,  Hg2
    Property: category
        Value: Abstract transfer

    Property: receivingChemicalName
        Value: Divalent Mercury
    Property: sendingChemicalName
        Value: Divalent Mercury

    Property: doesTransformChemical
        Value: false
    Property: doesTransportChemical
        Value: true
    Property: enabled
        Value: true
    Property: isDefaultForCategory
        Value: true
    Property: receivingCompartmentCategory
        Value: Terrestrial Plant | Leaf
    Property: sendingCompartmentCategory
        Value: PseudoSource | Wet | Vapor

    Property: transferFactor
